nexu-io/open-design ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
open-design-v0.25.0Open Design 0.25.0 — Steadier Ground Stable2026-10-15T14:44:06Z

🌟 Codename: Steadier Ground

🧭 5 PRs · 2 contributors · 4 days — Open Design gets out of its own way. Old workspace activity and noisy support evidence used to linger after you had moved on → 0.25.0 keeps the signal with the work that still matters. 🚀

🔥 Highlights

  • 🗂️ Recent projects follow the workspace you are actually in. Switching catalogs used to leave an old project quietly polling in the background; now its stale activity steps aside, so the projects in front of you get the attention. (#8442) Thanks @Siri-Ray.
  • 🔎 Automatic diagnostics keep the useful trail, not the noise. When an AMR run fails, its runtime records can join the evidence; repeated delivery receipts no longer crowd out the failure you need to understand. (#8488, #8461) Thanks @Siri-Ray.
  • 🔐 Consent remains a real boundary on Windows. Rotated logs stay on the correct side of the choice you made, so support evidence remains helpful without widening what you agreed to share. (#8487) Thanks @Siri-Ray.

📥 Download: Tag open-design-v0.25.0.

Platform Architecture Assets
macOS Apple silicon DMG · SHA-256
macOS Intel DMG · SHA-256
Windows x64 Installer · SHA-256

🙏 Thanks to everyone who shipped 0.25.0

@Siri-Ray · @PerishCode

Yeachan-Heo/oh-my-codex ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.21.12 Stable2026-10-15T13:41:28Z

oh-my-codex v0.21.12

0.21.12 is a patch release for the frozen range v0.21.11..6937884f4d09a485d5b4f16498517bcd05384e06 (6 commits, 15 files, +998/−19).

Highlights

  • Claim-bound completion evidence (#3797): implementation tasks marked requires_code_change: true now require a report tied to the active task claim and a structured verification summary before completion. Existing clients must supply that evidence; tasks without the flag and transitions to failed retain their previous behavior.
  • More reliable HUD updates (#3786, #3791): watcher repair children are bounded so hung reconciliation cannot block later repairs, and git branch labels are cached until HEAD, refs, or git config changes.
  • Dependency update (#3794): update @modelcontextprotocol/sdk from 1.32.0 to 1.32.1.

Fixes and compatibility

  • Implementation-task clients completing tasks marked requires_code_change: true must write claim-bound evidence under .omx/evidence/ and include a structured verification summary and evidence marker. Non-code-change tasks and failed transitions do not require a report (#3797).
  • No breaking CLI or package-layout changes are included. HUD test fixture isolation improvements are covered by #3788 and #3789.

Validation evidence

Frozen candidate 6937884f4d09a485d5b4f16498517bcd05384e06 is green on dev CI. Local build, typecheck, and relevant test results are recorded in docs/qa/release-readiness-0.21.12.md.

Full readiness evidence: docs/qa/release-readiness-0.21.12.md.

Merged PR inventory

PR Author Title
#3786 @spicysharkco fix(hud): bound the watcher repair child so a hung reconcile cannot block repairs
#3788 @spicysharkco test(hud): put the real-tmux test socket under tmpdir
#3789 @spicysharkco test(hud): make the adaptive-budget resize watch test hermetic
#3791 @spicysharkco perf(hud): cache the git branch label until HEAD, refs or git config change
#3794 @dependabot build(deps): bump @modelcontextprotocol/sdk from 1.32.0 to 1.32.1
#3797 @shenyankm feat(team): Require completion evidence for implementation tasks

Contributors

Thanks to @clawdbot, @dependabot[bot], @shenyankm, @spicysharkco, and @Yeachan-Heo for contributing to this release.

Inventory

The reproducible range is recorded in artifacts/release-0.21.12/inventory.md.

deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness ↗ 1 release · 1 prerelease
dsh-v0.2.1-alpha.4v0.2.1-alpha.4 Prerelease2026-10-15T13:40:02Z

中文 | English

中文

新增功能

  • 自定义模型 API 支持通过UI中配置各模型的推理等级，无需手动修改配置文件。 by @LegGasai
  • 允许用户从“自定义插件索引源”中发现和安装插件。 by @turtle1999

问题修复

  • 修复命令与参数交界处输入异常的问题。支持整体选中/删除，并在撤销或刷新页面后恢复。 by @LegGasai
  • 改进 Office 文件交付：避免在回复中引用已删除的预览图，减少 PDF 导出后不必要的工具查找和额外检查。 by @yudshj
  • 修复启用开发者工具后，仍需刷新页面才能打开工具面板的问题。 by @imccyu
  • 减少 Linux 下启动命令前的重复检查，降低命令启动开销，并改善较旧 systemd 环境的兼容性。 by @imccyu
  • 修复命令执行失败时，未呈现有效的状态信息。 by @yudshj

其他变更

  • 第三方插件的版本兼容性优先按 engines.dsh 判断；未声明时沿用原有规则。插件作者需检查声明是否覆盖支持的 DSH 版本。 by @turtle1999

English

New Features

  • Configure reasoning levels for individual custom API models in the UI without editing configuration files manually. by @LegGasai
  • Discover and install plugins from custom plugin index sources. by @turtle1999

Bug Fixes

  • Fix input glitches between a command and its arguments. Commands can be selected and deleted as a whole, and restored after undo or a page reload. by @LegGasai
  • Improve Office file delivery: avoid referencing deleted preview images in replies and reduce unnecessary tool searches and extra checks after PDF export. by @yudshj
  • Fix Developer Tools requiring a page reload before its panel can open after being enabled. by @imccyu
  • Reduce repeated checks before launching commands on Linux, lowering startup overhead and improving compatibility with older systemd environments. by @imccyu
  • Fix failed commands not displaying useful status information. by @yudshj

Other Changes

  • Third-party plugin compatibility prioritizes engines.dsh when declared and otherwise uses the existing rules. Plugin authors should check that the declared range covers their supported DSH versions. by @turtle1999

Full Changelog: dsh-v0.2.1-alpha.3...dsh-v0.2.1-alpha.4

cua-spaces-v0.9.0Cua Spaces v0.9.0 Stable2026-10-14T19:44:12Z

Cua Spaces 0.9.0

Highlights

  • The New UI is on by default. The redesigned app (the same UI as the new Windows and Linux app) opens by default. You can switch back any time: Settings → Experiments → Use classic UI.
  • A real Teams screen. Settings → Teams lists your organizations, their members and pending invites. Manage team and Billing open the account app at app.cua.ai. The app now talks to api.cua.ai and app.cua.ai, and no longer calls the retired run.cua.ai.
  • Drop onto a Space. Drag a window onto a Space's tile to teleport it there, or drop files on a tile to send them to that Space.
  • Find things faster. A filter box on the Spaces page. ⌘K finds machines by name and keyword. Power errors now show on the Space's tile.

Fixes

  • The Get started checklist shows the same count in every UI, and the Machines page lists every machine.
  • Teleport: clearer words when a teleport is refused, the right title after Back, and the right Status for a Space that can't be reached.
  • Windows: sign-in, Docker detection, presence without Windows Hello, teleporting Edge, and wording fixes.
  • Linux: the Ubuntu .deb can be removed cleanly, onboarding images show, the skipped sign-in panel is fixed, and the Chrome sandbox works.
  • The Windows and Linux app ships on stable releases.

Full changelog

v0.8.161 Stable2026-10-15T09:12:23Z

HyperFrames v0.8.161

Released on 2026-10-15.

Find music beats without launching Chrome. TypeScript projects using NodeNext can now resolve the published package declarations.

Features

  • hyperframes beats uses the TypeScript beat tracker through FFmpeg decoding, without Chrome. Studio shares the tracker; low-confidence audio returns no beat grid. The saved beat-file format is unchanged. (#5634)

Fixes

  • Resolve published TypeScript declarations with NodeNext module resolution across packages. (#5532)

v0.8.160 Stable2026-10-15T06:07:13Z

HyperFrames v0.8.160

Released on 2026-10-15.

Measure sound timing with the new audio command. This release also improves trimmed-video previews, Windows rendering, Claude usage reporting, history saves, and element picking.

Features

  • Measure audio onsets, peaks, pauses, and loudness with hyperframes audio. Project queries report film time, including clip trims and playback rates. Timeline rows now expose mediaStart for consumers that map file timestamps into the film. (#5602)

Fixes

  • Prepare trimmed video frames before preview cuts, reducing flashes of the wrong frame. (#5633)
  • Keep history saves moving when a file changes while it is being copied. (#5627)
  • Show Claude plan spending even when the usage response has no rate windows. (#5629)
  • Let the first Windows render wait for newly installed browser and FFmpeg files to become accessible. (#5625)
  • Select content beneath faint SVG grain and keep page-wide CSS resets out of an element's inspected styles. (#5628)

Full changelog

v0.8.159 Stable2026-10-15T00:56:09Z

HyperFrames v0.8.159

Released on 2026-10-14.

check and snapshot now work in sandboxes that refuse to listen on localhost. snapshot --keep lets you redo one still without losing the others.

Features

  • CLI: snapshot --keep redoes one still and leaves the others (c0636d017f6, #5605). It adds to the output folder instead of clearing it and names each still for its time, such as at-1.5s.png.

Fixes

  • Core: A render's first frame shows each scene's starting state (8c00ebad80c, #5614).
  • Core: A hidden scene's contents stay hidden when a child is set visible (24a9b781715, #5619). Before, a nested scene's card could paint before its host started. The static-volume-future-set regression baseline changes for this reason.
  • CLI: check and snapshot run where a sandbox refuses to listen on localhost (71595dd8201, #5618).
  • CLI: The GPU check uses the same pipe as the render, so a sandboxed check no longer waits 120 s (6c89decb0dd, #5622).
  • CLI: A long video conversion in init never stalls on ffmpeg's own log (b14a4564716, #5616).

Internal

  • Core: The var() font fallback test uses an offline fetch stub instead of Google Fonts (8f0bcadcd32, #5623).
  • A stalled history fetch in change detection is retried instead of failing the run (cd87cf11106, #5615).

Full changelog

https://github.com/heygen-com/hyperframes/compare/v0.8.158...v0.8.159

View release →

v0.8.158 Stable2026-10-14T19:46:32Z

HyperFrames v0.8.158

Released on 2026-10-14.

hyperframes init --keep-original brings a clip into a project as it is, without converting it. Storyboards are now one board of stills, and check no longer fails on a busy Windows machine.

Features

  • CLI: init --keep-original copies a clip as it is, without transcoding (d65eefad5ed, #5612).
  • Skills: Storyboards are a board of stills with one page format (6f90ca15a7f, #5599). Agents pose each shot, snapshot it, and lay the stills out on one page, boards/storyboard.html, before animating.

Fixes

  • CLI: Capture keeps fonts a page declares in its own style block (4957bf6939a, #5609).
  • CLI: A killed browser on a busy Windows machine no longer fails check (fe29f157f94, #5607). Windows reports the process gone at once but its exit seconds later, and check used to stop on that gap.

Internal

  • Engine: The warm extraction cache test checks ffmpeg did not run, not the clock (14e2ccf3bb0, #5613).
  • Engine: Audio effect tests that start Chrome get the same time as the others (7744692a464, #5608).

Full changelog

https://github.com/heygen-com/hyperframes/compare/v0.8.157...v0.8.158

openclaw/openclaw ↗ 2 releases · 1 stable · 1 prerelease
v2026.9.33openclaw 2026.9.33 Stable2026-10-15T10:39:22Z

This is a gateway-only extended-stable release, which is our current equivalent to LTS. This release is OpenClaw from the end of September 2026, plus critical security updates, reliability and performance fixes, and features like new model support. The latest version of OpenClaw at the time of this release is 2026.10.3

2026.9.33

Highlights

  • Safer September upgrades: updates on the 2026.9 line keep state, plugin data, service ownership, and Windows state paths intact, and refuse automatic restore over unattributed Doctor writes. (#160167, #166755) Thanks @RomneyDa, @vyctorbrzezowski, @jalehman, @Alix-007, @jayzhou2309, @nitinjwadhawan, @SunnyShu0925, @obviyus, @vincentkoc, @taihartman, and @DonnieFi.
  • Updates that finish on pnpm, npm, and Windows: pnpm 12 Gateway services and launchers survive native updates, retained updater runtimes no longer block npm recovery, and Windows updates no longer roll back after Doctor rechecks driver ancestry. (#170003, #170131, #170170, #169563) Thanks @xilopaint, @k-sekula, @Warhangel, @RomneyDa, @DonnieFi, @Takhoffman, @NianJiuZst, @masatohoshino, @obviyus, @ericcurtin, @jjjhenriksen, @sjf-oa, @ceckert, @jayzhou2309, @brokemac79, @Yolo521-bot, @mightyx, and @ajmtrz.
  • New model support: run Meta Muse Wonder, use MiniMax M3.1 thinking effort levels, and stay signed in to MiniMax portal OAuth past token expiry. (#170232, #160089, #160459) Thanks @RomneyDa, @NianJiuZst, @Takhoffman, @php737, @masatohoshino, @rongtianhua, @DonnieFi, @obviyus, @ericcurtin, @jjjhenriksen, @sjf-oa, @ceckert, @jayzhou2309, @brokemac79, @xilopaint, @k-sekula, @Warhangel, @Yolo521-bot, @mightyx, and @ajmtrz.
  • More reliable model turns: agent runs honor live thinking levels, local providers get response-header deadlines, ChatGPT WebSocket normal closes recover, and Codex replies keep their final tokens. (#168566, #168282, #167512, #168400) Thanks @obviyus, @ericcurtin, @mightyx, @RomneyDa, @DonnieFi, @Takhoffman, @NianJiuZst, @masatohoshino, @jjjhenriksen, @sjf-oa, @ceckert, @jayzhou2309, @brokemac79, @xilopaint, @k-sekula, @Warhangel, @Yolo521-bot, and @ajmtrz.
  • Work survives Gateway restarts: interrupted automation turns, steered sessions, cloud worker claims, and partial worker replies now resume or persist after a restart. (#170162, #170252, #170161, #170223) Thanks @DonnieFi, @Takhoffman, @NianJiuZst, @RomneyDa, @masatohoshino, @obviyus, @ericcurtin, @jjjhenriksen, @sjf-oa, @ceckert, @jayzhou2309, @brokemac79, @xilopaint, @k-sekula, @Warhangel, @Yolo521-bot, @mightyx, and @ajmtrz.
  • Delivery and device stability: live sends survive queue recovery, and node requests no longer fail on unrelated pairing activity or while another device's pairing is written. (#169010, #169486, #169575) Thanks @RomneyDa, @DonnieFi, @Takhoffman, @NianJiuZst, @masatohoshino, @obviyus, @ericcurtin, @jjjhenriksen, @sjf-oa, @ceckert, @jayzhou2309, @brokemac79, @xilopaint, @k-sekula, @Warhangel, @Yolo521-bot, @mightyx, and @ajmtrz.
  • Agent and update stability: carry a broad batch of compatible October fixes for agent turns, sessions, channels, and update recovery. (#170476) Thanks @RomneyDa, @yetval, @Dimitri-lcht, @Program-123, @dhruv1220, @Shenrui-Ma, @igs-rogenlo, @changeroa, @hxy91819, @Takhoffman, @harjothkhara, @TYY-2005, @giodl73-repo, @masatohoshino, @obviyus, @jalehman, @wangmiao0668000666, and @an2in.

Changes

  • Meta: add the Muse Wonder model to the catalog and onboarding with max thinking support, keeping Spark as the default. (#170232) Thanks @RomneyDa, @DonnieFi, @Takhoffman, @NianJiuZst, @masatohoshino, @obviyus, @ericcurtin, @jjjhenriksen, @sjf-oa, @ceckert, @jayzhou2309, @brokemac79, @xilopaint, @k-sekula, @Warhangel, @Yolo521-bot, @mightyx, and @ajmtrz.
  • MiniMax: add the MiniMax M3.1 Flash Preview model and route its thinking effort levels to the provider. (#160089) Thanks @NianJiuZst, @Takhoffman, @php737, @DonnieFi, @RomneyDa, @masatohoshino, @obviyus, @ericcurtin, @jjjhenriksen, @sjf-oa, @ceckert, @jayzhou2309, @brokemac79, @xilopaint, @k-sekula, @Warhangel, @Yolo521-bot, @mightyx, and @ajmtrz.
  • LM Studio: refreshing saved inventory now discovers newly loaded models. (#168039) Thanks @jb-lopez, @DonnieFi, @Takhoffman, @NianJiuZst, @RomneyDa, @masatohoshino, @obviyus, @ericcurtin, @jjjhenriksen, @sjf-oa, @ceckert, @jayzhou2309, @brokemac79, @xilopaint, @k-sekula, @Warhangel, @Yolo521-bot, @mightyx, and @ajmtrz.

Fixes

  • Update and Doctor: keep Doctor running under unreadable supervisors and child binding errors, keep clean service install refusals out of native-authority recovery, stop update history writes from stalling cron and cleanup, avoid bookkeeping-only repair refusals, and never delete an OAuth profile because the legacy auth store is unreadable. (#169665, #169658, #169283, #170195, #170256) Thanks @DonnieFi, @Yolo521-bot, @Takhoffman, @NianJiuZst, @RomneyDa, @masatohoshino, @obviyus, @ericcurtin, @jjjhenriksen, @sjf-oa, @ceckert, @jayzhou2309, @brokemac79, @xilopaint, @k-sekula, @Warhangel, @mightyx, and @ajmtrz.
  • Updating from 2026.8.35: cron jobs approved with "allow always" keep running unattended after the update, pnpm 12 Gateway services left on a deleted store generation are rewritten to the durable install link, Doctor defers optional checks so updates finish under older updaters' time limits, official plugins with removed SDK imports are no longer silently kept, and Kubernetes pods run Doctor before starting. (#171816) Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • Gateway responsiveness: long streamed replies that start with a reply tag such as [[reply_to_current]] no longer re-parse the whole reply on every delta, which could keep the Gateway busy for tens of seconds. #166781 (#166782) Thanks @boeingchoco and @obviyus.
  • Tailscale: an interrupted Tailscale Serve start stops its serve process, the Gateway keeps local access when the tailnet denies Serve, and public chat no longer returns 503s right after startup. #167436 (#167514, #171509, #167604) Thanks @pandysp and @RomneyDa.
  • Context engine: sessions holding an old blocked turn no longer repeat the blocked-turn warning and re-read it on every run. (#167595) Thanks @jalehman.
  • State database: state reads no longer stay closed until a Gateway restart after a failed state cleanup. Fixes #162085. (#171709) Thanks @RomneyDa and @residentb2b.
  • Update summaries and Doctor: successful updates stop listing settled plugin data and settings upgrades as pending, optional dreaming-cron inspection or snapshot cleanup failures no longer block updates, and macOS LaunchAgent repair accepts an in-progress bootstrap. (#170413, #170754, #170740) Thanks @RomneyDa and @DonnieFi.
  • Providers: recover DeepSeek calls markup instead of leaking tool payloads, recover Codex follow-up requests after failed thread preparation, and select aliased llama.cpp models while failing setup when credentials cannot be saved. (#169728, #169431, #168232) Thanks @TOMUIV, @jjjhenriksen, @DonnieFi, @Takhoffman, @NianJiuZst, @RomneyDa, @masatohoshino, @obviyus, @ericcurtin, @sjf-oa, @ceckert, @jayzhou2309, @brokemac79, @xilopaint, @k-sekula, @Warhangel, @Yolo521-bot, @mightyx, and @ajmtrz.
  • Channels and tools: channel tools keep working after a config write restarts the account, ACP agents keep plugin and OpenClaw tools when acpx is installed separately, Talk consultations no longer abort right after connecting, and routed turns deliver blocking questions. (#164848, #159396, #164272, #170083, #170039) Thanks @sjf-oa, @ceckert, @jayzhou2309, @ajmtrz, @brokemac79, @DonnieFi, @Takhoffman, @NianJiuZst, @RomneyDa, @masatohoshino, @obviyus, @ericcurtin, @jjjhenriksen, @xilopaint, @k-sekula, @Warhangel, @Yolo521-bot, and @mightyx.
  • Automation: failed one-shot automations always alert, and cron no longer holds the state writer while waiting on host admission. (#169102, #168291) Thanks @DonnieFi, @Takhoffman, @NianJiuZst, @RomneyDa, @masatohoshino, @obviyus, @ericcurtin, @jjjhenriksen, @sjf-oa, @ceckert, @jayzhou2309, @brokemac79, @xilopaint, @k-sekula, @Warhangel, @Yolo521-bot, @mightyx, and @ajmtrz.
  • Release checks: Windows installer checks tolerate slow gateway stop help and surface cleanup errors, packaged checks recover from a busy Gateway port, and extended-stable candidates now qualify upgrades from the previous extended-stable release. (#169567, #170178, #171565) Thanks @RomneyDa, @DonnieFi, @Takhoffman, @NianJiuZst, @masatohoshino, @obviyus, @ericcurtin, @jjjhenriksen, @sjf-oa, @ceckert, @jayzhou2309, @brokemac79, @xilopaint, @k-sekula, @Warhangel, @Yolo521-bot, @mightyx, and @ajmtrz.

v2026.10.5-beta.4openclaw 2026.10.5-beta.4 Prerelease2026-10-15T06:10:42Z

2026.10.5-beta.4

Highlights

  • Updates finish more reliably: macOS updates no longer time out on aliased temporary directories, systemd user installs reclaim abandoned runtimes, pacman and FreeBSD package installs are left to their package manager, snapshot failures are identified before activation with accurate recovery advice, and the success summary no longer repeats settled plugin upgrades as pending. (#169860, #171488, #169055, #171457, #170351, #170413) Thanks @RomneyDa, @DonnieFi, and @Pegorim.
  • ChatGPT models work on the default Codex runtime: ChatGPT-listed models no longer fail before sending any request. (#168033) Thanks @obviyus.
  • Headless browser sign-ins stay usable: headless pages keep accepting clicks and key presses while a site waits for a passkey. (#170857) Thanks @trukhinyuri.

Changes

  • Context limits: status shows corrected context limits after model discovery, Copilot honors reported context capacity when native limits are missing, and llama.cpp keeps live router model limits and tools on refresh. (#165991, #156636, #168309) Thanks @snotty, @saju01, and @RomneyDa.

Fixes

  • Gateway access: local access keeps working when the tailnet denies Tailscale Serve, and lifecycle publications survive after the emitting producer drains. (#171509, #170231) Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • macOS repair and recovery: update recovery finishes repairing the bundled Gateway, the app keeps a compatible newer Gateway during recovery instead of downgrading it, LaunchAgent repair accepts verified bootstrap recovery, and Doctor allows offline state repair for app-owned Gateways. (#171508, #170766, #170740, #170768) Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • Update resilience: updates keep moving when optional cron inspection or snapshot cleanup fails. (#170754) Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • Security: Tlon never treats group DM authors as the owner. (#165801) Thanks @eleqtrizit.
  • Turns and replies: Claude CLI sessions remember announced sub-agent results on the next turn, agent plugin reloads no longer wait on their own turn, interrupted turns are preserved when new messages arrive, ACP follow-up replies no longer disappear after the previous turn is stopped, context-engine advancement survives steering, visible sub-agent spawns try the primary model first, and launched agent turns no longer fail with writer claim errors. (#164175, #171257, #170647, #170883, #168394, #169984, #170077) Thanks @robertoronderosjr, @RomneyDa, @etzelm, and @jacobtomlinson.
  • Gateway lifecycle: the Gateway stays responsive during reply-tagged streams and long quoted tool XML no longer triggers per-token Markdown parsing, blocked context-engine turns stop repeating in the Gateway log on every run, the Control UI no longer reconnects on delayed heartbeats, state reads stay closed until restart after a failed state cleanup, the Gateway recovers state ownership after sleep and clock changes, stalled transcript workers no longer block shutdown, database admission no longer blocks worker startup, shutdown joins lifecycle publication, forced descendant cleanup releases worker capacity, enabled standalone plugin files start with the Gateway, Gateway restart works from verified external macOS terminals, and Android/Termux gateways no longer fail with "shared-state database generation changed". (#166782, #171573, #167595, #171253, #171709, #170790, #170493, #170348, #170555, #168746, #171330, #170354, #167719) Thanks @aniruddhaadak80, @holgergruenhagen, @obviyus, @RomneyDa, @yxshee, @grabowskidaniel359-design, @jjjhenriksen, @AnvinaLI, @sgh6688, @waynegault, @Oldrich333, @residentb2b, @boeingchoco, and @jalehman.
  • Workers: worker heap limits are enforced under process-wide Node flags. (#166581) Thanks @valkyriweb, @xydigitLybnnnn, @goslingmanagment, and @waynegault.
  • Channels and tools: MCP OAuth login completes with portless loopback registrations, and each Feishu monitor start gets its own binding adapter registration. (#170876, #169226) Thanks @9fw2pq8sgb-art, @ruel225, @obviyus, and @junniar.
  • Update and Doctor: update repair no longer fails after Doctor completes on busy macOS hosts, slow update admission no longer holds the database writer and disrupts Gateway work, update repair prepares its config before inference, metadata inventory survives progress-output backpressure, Docker upgrades stop retry loops on older Linux hosts, Doctor explains plugin migration backup coverage, and Doctor no longer logs spurious WAL maintenance warnings while repairing shared state. (#170779, #171493, #170276, #168493, #162305, #169295, #170783) Thanks @vincentkoc, @yxshee, @Ludwigtheo0815, @RomneyDa, and @DonnieFi.
  • Plugin publishing: plugin npm publication no longer fails when artifact pagination repeats an entry. (#171044) Thanks @RomneyDa.

NousResearch/hermes-agent ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v0.22.2Hermes Agent v0.22.2 Stable2026-10-15T08:21:32Z

Hermes Agent v0.22.2

Release Date: October 15, 2026

v0.22.1 was built from the wrong commit. It shipped unreleased gateway work, and the Microsoft Store app could stick on "Connecting." 0.22.2 is the release 0.22.1 was meant to be.

Known issue: Sessions that ran under the 0.22.1 gateway can't be deleted in 0.22.2.

<!-- HERMES_BUILDS_TABLE -->

Highlights

  • The gateway starts after a reboot again. After a restart, Linux can hand an early PID to an unrelated process, and the gateway could mistake it for a running gateway, so a supervised gateway (systemd, launchd, Docker) refused to start. Hermes now records the boot it started in, and when it can't tell, it asks the host lock, which only a live gateway holds. (#138594, fixes #133744)
  • Long local-model turns survive on laptops. Local requests always stream with prompt-reading progress (shown in the status line), stall timers skip time the machine was asleep, a recovered local server gets another try instead of a breaker that failed every later message, and compression no longer times out while the server is still reading a long conversation. On Windows unified-memory machines, model launch and the prompt cache are sized to free memory. (#138560)

Below is everything in the 0.22 release line since v0.22.0: the fixes 0.22.1 was meant to ship, plus the two above. Work that is only on main ships with v0.23.0.

oomol-lab/open-connector ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v1.9.0 Stable2026-10-15T03:40:05Z

Upgrade notice: Shopify migration required

v1.9.0 includes breaking changes for Shopify integrations. Review existing Shopify workflows before upgrading.

  • The shopify_admin provider has been removed and consolidated into shopify. Update service references and configure a connection under shopify; there is no compatibility alias for the old service name.
  • For API-key connections, use the Shopify Admin API access token and the shopDomain field (for example, acme.myshopify.com). OAuth client configuration uses shopSubdomain (for example, acme). Check that the connection has the scopes required by the actions you use.
  • Re-read each action's input and output schemas before migrating. GraphQL actions use string global IDs such as gid://shopify/Product/123, cursor inputs such as first and after, and pageInfo pagination metadata. The get_shop action now returns normalized GraphQL shop fields, so existing REST response field mappings need updating.
  • REST content actions for blogs, pages, and articles retain numeric IDs and limit / page_info pagination. Apply the schema for the specific action rather than converting every Shopify action to GraphQL parameters.

See Shopify consolidation and expanded actions and the current Shopify action definitions.

Highlights

  • Added 53 provider definitions since v1.8.0, including QuickBooks Online, LiteLLM, GreyNoise, Opper, ScanMalware, Kagi, and API Route.
  • Expanded existing integrations, including Gmail attachments and CID inline images, Shopify Admin operations, WhatsApp analytics, and Feishu workflows.
  • Improved OAuth reconnect reporting, refresh lifetime handling, and protection against concurrent disconnects; added optional consent scopes, optional token revocation, and a configurable completion-page close delay.
  • Moved downloads across many providers to streaming transit storage and fixed transit cleanup, multipart byte limits, and malformed Unicode filenames.
  • Improved runtime error envelopes, generated action examples, provider error handling, and dependency security fixes.

What's Changed

  • feat(providers): add Cheaper Inference by @aiapienthusiast in https://github.com/oomol-lab/open-connector/pull/636
  • fix(marketplace): load default discovery through runtime by @Harbor404 in https://github.com/oomol-lab/open-connector/pull/637
  • feat(oauth): revocationUrl, and revoke on disconnect when asked by @RoryCai92 in https://github.com/oomol-lab/open-connector/pull/634
  • fix(retell-ai): migrate agent listing and repair call filters by @hyrious in https://github.com/oomol-lab/open-connector/pull/640
  • fix(firecrawl): support self-hosted instance base URL by @monody0007 in https://github.com/oomol-lab/open-connector/pull/625
  • fix(googledrive): classify rate-limit 403s as rate_limited by @powerfulZ in https://github.com/oomol-lab/open-connector/pull/631
  • feat: add opt-in admission hooks for shared provider HTTP by @l1shen in https://github.com/oomol-lab/open-connector/pull/643
  • fix: preserve API error contracts for HTTP admission denials by @l1shen in https://github.com/oomol-lab/open-connector/pull/644
  • feat(providers): add GetYouTubeTranscript provider by @pushkarsingh32 in https://github.com/oomol-lab/open-connector/pull/646

esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v2.41.0Reasonix CLI v2.41.0 Stable2026-10-15T00:57:37Z

CLI archives built from the Studio release studio-v2.41.0.

This release adds side questions, keeping a long paste as a file, optional session-row buttons and a sandbox device allowlist, and fixes narrow-window layout, contrast, memory recall and remote-control compatibility.

New

  • Settings → Model preferences gains an independent naming model and per-role reasoning effort, so titles can use their own model and each role its own effort. Refs #11851.
  • New side questions: ask a read-only question beside the main conversation; read-only tools, never in the main transcript, dropped on close. Refs #12927, #13128.
  • Each class in the context details can be opened to its largest items (sizes and tool names only, never message text). Refs #13052, #13143.
  • When an MCP server puts ttlMs on its tool list, the catalog is re-read once it runs out (clamped to 30 s to 1 h) instead of relying on change notices alone. Refs #13108.
  • A skill's allowed-tools now also takes the open-standard spelling (space separated, like Bash(git add:*) Read); commas still work. Refs #13110.
  • reasonix doctor now reports skill names and descriptions that depart from the Agent Skills spec (uppercase, hyphens, length, directory mismatch); such skills still load. Refs #13110.
  • Settings → Appearance gains "Session row buttons": when on, hovering a sidebar session row shows Archive and Delete; off by default, both stay in the context menu. Refs #13132.
  • When compaction rejects its candidate as too large, the message now splits it into fixed prefix, protected messages, summary and recent tail, not just "still above trigger". Refs #13148.
  • The feedback dialog gains an "Ideas and discussion" link to GitHub Discussions for suggestions; bugs and errors still use Send. Refs #13141, #13155.
  • New [sandbox] allow_devices: the Linux command sandbox hides host devices such as the GPU; a user-level allowlist binds them in. Off by default; project config cannot set it. Refs #13157.
  • The long-paste chip gains "save as file": on click the text is stored as a file and the message carries only a pointer (lines, size, path, how to read it); never automatic. Refs #13148, #13162.
  • In the terminal, a folded long paste can be kept as a file with Ctrl+G: the message carries a pointer, not the text; without the key it is sent whole as before. Refs #13148, #13166.

Changed

  • ACP tool-call updates now name the invoked tool. Refs #13114, #13130.
  • On Linux, bubblewrap installed but unable to create a namespace (containers, hardened kernels) now gets its real cause and ways out, not "install it". Refs #13117, #13138, #13146.
  • Compaction now refuses up front when the protected messages and recent tail already reach the trigger, naming what holds the size, instead of paying for a summary that cannot fit. Refs #13148.

Fixed

  • A recalled memory fact edited or deleted outside the session is re-read before the next recall (only the last hits are checked, no full scan). Refs #12948.
  • Unreadable memory directories now report errors, keeping revision lists, restores, and recovery from treating permission or storage failures as missing data. Refs #12950.
  • On Linux, a launch folder nobody picked and nothing was said in is no longer listed as a project, so deleting it does not bring it back on restart. Refs #13107, #13127.
  • A requested file that does not exist is now said so in the side panel instead of opening a blank preview page. Refs #13125, #13133.
  • With a wallpaper set, the sidebar footer no longer paints a whiter plate; the picture continues to the bottom. Refs #13129, #13131.
  • On a phone browser too old for X25519 in WebCrypto, remote control now falls back to an audited software curve, loaded only then (about 24 KB), instead of asking for a newer browser. Refs #13140.
  • When an older session cannot be opened after a restart, the error now says why: the file is gone, its folder is gone, or the system could not read the path (with its reason). Refs #13152, #13159.
  • Narrow windows no longer overlap or squeeze: the tab bar wraps, settings labels keep their width, segmented controls stay in the sheet, and a few colours reach AA. Refs #13169.
  • Narrow windows: activity step rows keep their duration and arrow, the meter rail's subagent chip keeps its count when it cannot fit, and config error paths wrap. Refs #13154, #13172.
  • Fixed two contrast shortfalls: the intro "press any key to skip" hint and the +/− counts in the changed-files pill (both themes). Found by the audit at f8c262da0.

studio-v2.41.0Reasonix Studio v2.41.0 Stable2026-10-15T00:55:19Z

本版本新增辅助会话、按需保存长粘贴、会话行快捷按钮、沙盒设备白名单等功能，并修复窄窗口布局、对比度、记忆召回和远控兼容等一批问题。

新增

  • 设置 → 模型偏好新增「命名模型」和按角色的推理强度：可以单独指定用哪个模型给会话起标题，并分别设置各角色的推理强度。关联 #11851 by @Guan0923。
  • 新增「辅助会话」：在主对话旁边问一个只读的小问题，用只读工具回答，不写进主对话、不改文件，关闭即丢弃。关联 #12927 fixed in #13128、#13128。
  • 上下文详情里每一类占用可以展开，看最大的几项（只显示大小和工具名，不含消息内容）。关联 #13052、#13143。
  • MCP 服务器在工具列表响应里给出 ttlMs 时，工具目录会在到期后自动重读（限制在 30 秒到 1 小时之间），不再只靠变更通知。关联 #13108。
  • 技能的 allowed-tools 现在也接受开放标准写法（空格分隔，如 Bash(git add:*) Read），原来的逗号分隔照常可用；括号内的分隔符属于同一条规则。关联 #13110。
  • reasonix doctor 现在会提示技能的名称、描述不符合 Agent Skills 开放标准（大写、连字符、超长、与目录名不一致），这些技能仍会正常加载。关联 #13110。
  • 设置 → 外观新增「会话行快捷按钮」：打开后，鼠标移到侧栏会话行上会显示「归档」「删除」按钮；默认关闭，两个操作仍在右键菜单里。关联 #13132。
  • 压缩被拒时，提示现在列出候选内容的构成（固定前缀、被保护的消息、摘要、最近回合各占多少），不再只说「候选仍高于触发线」。关联 #13148。
  • 反馈对话框新增「想法与讨论」入口，建议和想法可以发到 GitHub 讨论区，bug 和错误仍用发送。关联 #13141、#13155。
  • 新增 [sandbox] allow_devices：Linux 命令沙盒默认看不到宿主的设备节点（如显卡），现在可在用户级配置里用 /dev/nvidia* 这类白名单放进去；默认关闭，项目配置不能开。关联 #13157。
  • 撰写框里的长粘贴芯片新增「存为文件」：点了才把这段文字存成文件，消息里只带一段指针（行数、大小、路径、怎么读），原文不再随每个请求发送；不会自动存。关联 #13148、#13162。
  • 终端界面里折叠的长粘贴可以按 Ctrl+G 存成文件：发送时只带一段指针，不再把原文送给模型；不按就和以前一样整段发送。关联 #13148、#13166。

变更

  • ACP 的工具调用更新现在带上被调用的工具名。关联 #13114、#13130。
  • Linux 上 bubblewrap 已安装却不能创建命名空间（容器、受限内核）时，提示不再说「请安装」，而是说明真实原因和出路。关联 #13117、#13138、#13146。
  • 压缩前先预判：被保护的消息和最近回合本身就已超过触发线、摘要无论怎样都放不进去时，直接拒绝并说明构成，不再先花一次摘要调用。关联 #13148。

修复

  • 会话里召回过的记忆在会话外被修改或删除后，下一次召回会读到新内容（只重查上次命中的几条，不全量扫描）。关联 #12948 fixed in #13147。
  • 记忆目录因权限或存储故障无法读取时，现在明确报错，避免漏列历史版本、误报版本不存在，或根据不完整历史计算恢复版本号。关联 #12950 fixed in #13016 by @SivanCola。
  • Linux 上启动时不再把没人选过、也没说过话的启动目录（主目录）列成项目，删掉它重启后不会再回来。关联 #13107 fixed in #13127、#13127。
  • 要打开的文件不存在时，右侧面板现在明确提示「找不到该文件」，不再打开一片空白的预览页。关联 #13125、#13133。
  • 设置壁纸后，侧栏底部（钱包与用量、发送反馈、账号）不再多出一块发白的底，壁纸一直延续到最下面。关联 #13129、#13131。
  • 手机浏览器版本太旧、没有 X25519 加密时，远控现在改用经审计的软件实现继续连接（只在需要时才加载，约 24 KB），不再要求换浏览器。关联 #13140。
  • 重启后点以前的会话打不开时，提示现在区分原因：会话文件已不在、会话所在目录不在、或系统读不了该路径（并带上系统给的原因），不再一律说「无法解析该会话路径」。关联 #13152、#13159。
  • 窄窗口下界面不再重叠或被挤压：标签栏会换行，设置里的标签不再被压成一字一行，分段控件不再跑出面板，几处文字对比度提高到 AA。关联 #13169。
  • 窄窗口下的排版修正：活动步骤行的耗时与箭头不再被挤出，计量栏的子代理芯片收起文字保留数字，配置错误框的路径改为换行。关联 #13154、#13172。
  • 修复两处对比度不达 AA：开场页的「按任意键跳过」提示，和输入框上方「已更改文件」胶囊里的 +/− 数字（深浅两种主题）。整机审查于 f8c262da0。

贡献者

感谢本版本的贡献者：@Guan0923、@SivanCola

<!-- lang:en -->

This release adds side questions, keeping a long paste as a file, optional session-row buttons and a sandbox device allowlist, and fixes narrow-window layout, contrast, memory recall and remote-control compatibility.

New

  • Settings → Model preferences gains an independent naming model and per-role reasoning effort, so titles can use their own model and each role its own effort. Refs #11851 by @Guan0923.
  • New side questions: ask a read-only question beside the main conversation; read-only tools, never in the main transcript, dropped on close. Refs #12927 fixed in #13128, #13128.
  • Each class in the context details can be opened to its largest items (sizes and tool names only, never message text). Refs #13052, #13143.
  • When an MCP server puts ttlMs on its tool list, the catalog is re-read once it runs out (clamped to 30 s to 1 h) instead of relying on change notices alone. Refs #13108.
  • A skill's allowed-tools now also takes the open-standard spelling (space separated, like Bash(git add:*) Read); commas still work. Refs #13110.
  • reasonix doctor now reports skill names and descriptions that depart from the Agent Skills spec (uppercase, hyphens, length, directory mismatch); such skills still load. Refs #13110.
  • Settings → Appearance gains "Session row buttons": when on, hovering a sidebar session row shows Archive and Delete; off by default, both stay in the context menu. Refs #13132.
  • When compaction rejects its candidate as too large, the message now splits it into fixed prefix, protected messages, summary and recent tail, not just "still above trigger". Refs #13148.
  • The feedback dialog gains an "Ideas and discussion" link to GitHub Discussions for suggestions; bugs and errors still use Send. Refs #13141, #13155.
  • New [sandbox] allow_devices: the Linux command sandbox hides host devices such as the GPU; a user-level allowlist binds them in. Off by default; project config cannot set it. Refs #13157.
  • The long-paste chip gains "save as file": on click the text is stored as a file and the message carries only a pointer (lines, size, path, how to read it); never automatic. Refs #13148, #13162.
  • In the terminal, a folded long paste can be kept as a file with Ctrl+G: the message carries a pointer, not the text; without the key it is sent whole as before. Refs #13148, #13166.

Changed

  • ACP tool-call updates now name the invoked tool. Refs #13114, #13130.
  • On Linux, bubblewrap installed but unable to create a namespace (containers, hardened kernels) now gets its real cause and ways out, not "install it". Refs #13117, #13138, #13146.
  • Compaction now refuses up front when the protected messages and recent tail already reach the trigger, naming what holds the size, instead of paying for a summary that cannot fit. Refs #13148.

Fixed

  • A recalled memory fact edited or deleted outside the session is re-read before the next recall (only the last hits are checked, no full scan). Refs #12948 fixed in #13147.
  • Unreadable memory directories now report errors, keeping revision lists, restores, and recovery from treating permission or storage failures as missing data. Refs #12950 fixed in #13016 by @SivanCola.
  • On Linux, a launch folder nobody picked and nothing was said in is no longer listed as a project, so deleting it does not bring it back on restart. Refs #13107 fixed in #13127, #13127.
  • A requested file that does not exist is now said so in the side panel instead of opening a blank preview page. Refs #13125, #13133.
  • With a wallpaper set, the sidebar footer no longer paints a whiter plate; the picture continues to the bottom. Refs #13129, #13131.
  • On a phone browser too old for X25519 in WebCrypto, remote control now falls back to an audited software curve, loaded only then (about 24 KB), instead of asking for a newer browser. Refs #13140.
  • When an older session cannot be opened after a restart, the error now says why: the file is gone, its folder is gone, or the system could not read the path (with its reason). Refs #13152, #13159.
  • Narrow windows no longer overlap or squeeze: the tab bar wraps, settings labels keep their width, segmented controls stay in the sheet, and a few colours reach AA. Refs #13169.
  • Narrow windows: activity step rows keep their duration and arrow, the meter rail's subagent chip keeps its count when it cannot fit, and config error paths wrap. Refs #13154, #13172.
  • Fixed two contrast shortfalls: the intro "press any key to skip" hint and the +/− counts in the changed-files pill (both themes). Found by the audit at f8c262da0.

三个平台都能自更新：Linux 的 .deb 走自己的包（.rpm 的自更新尚未提供，请手动下载新包），Windows 运行下一个安装器，macOS 替换自身 bundle。

Fission-AI/OpenSpec ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v1.14.2v1.14.2 - Extension metadata, faster completion Stable2026-10-15T00:15:19Z

What's New in v1.14.2

This release lets you keep your own metadata on a change, makes Tab completion faster, and closes gaps where archive and verify could lose or miss content.

New

  • Extension keys in .openspec.yaml - Top-level keys starting with a lowercase x- (for example x-goal: G-12 or x-jiraKey) are yours for tracker ids, owners, or other tool data. OpenSpec ignores them, so openspec validate --strict passes on a change that carries them. Any other unrecognized key is still reported.

Improved

  • Faster Tab completion - Shell completion now loads 48 modules instead of 344 when completing changes or specs, and no longer records usage, so a Tab press never waits on telemetry.
  • Warnings for text archive drops - openspec validate and openspec archive now warn when a delta spec holds text that archive does not carry into the main spec, such as an extra ## section or prose before the first requirement. Like other warnings, these fail openspec validate --strict.
  • Verify checks removed requirements properly - /opsx:verify reads a REMOVED requirement's original text from the main spec before searching the code, and reports the entry as not verified when that text cannot be recovered, instead of passing it.

Fixed

  • Archive with symlinked directories - openspec archive accepts openspec/, openspec/changes, or openspec/specs as a symlink, for example to a shared docs repository.
  • Explore with custom schemas - Explore no longer offers to create default-schema files such as design.md under a schema that does not define them.
  • Update with glob artifacts - The update workflow no longer refuses to create a new file under a partly written glob artifact.
  • Deeply nested brace patterns - An artifact generates or apply.tracks pattern nesting braces more than 16 levels deep is rejected with a clear error instead of risking a crash.

New Contributors

  • @gliptak made their first contribution in https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/pull/981
  • @myspec-dev1 made their first contribution in https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/pull/2035
  • @Khunjua made their first contribution in https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/pull/2081
  • @PiPlusTheta made their first contribution in https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/pull/2094
  • @gkinter made their first contribution in https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/pull/2063

Full Changelog: https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/compare/v1.14.1...v1.14.2

v0.98.1 Stable2026-10-15T00:11:21Z

Faster idle sessions and richer responses

Highlights

  • Reduce idle CPU and disk reads in repositories with many untracked files
  • Share model catalog processing across equivalent clients while keeping usage and account state fresh
  • Allow Desktop-capable clients to receive Markdown responses through the new user_response tool

Improvements

  • Show compact context usage before and after compaction
  • Open the resume picker immediately while session scanning continues in the background
  • Disable automatic memory recall while retaining explicit memory search
  • Display large recorded token and cost summaries compactly

Fixes

  • Recover browser handoffs from runtime provider failures using direct browser actions
  • Restore Windows companion connections over named pipes
  • Expose global user-session connection presence to SDK clients
  • Keep worker emoji placement consistent in the swarm status strip

Windows signature status

Windows x86_64 and ARM64 executables are unsigned. Azure Artifact Signing is not configured and the repository's existing WINDOWS_SIGNING_REQUIRED=false override allowed unsigned publication. Windows may show publisher or SmartScreen warnings. SHA-256 checksums are provided for download integrity.

What's Changed

Graphify-Labs/graphify ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.9.910.9.91 Stable2026-10-14T22:17:45Z

Eight community pull requests: a Windows MCP-stdio hang fix, Rust/JS/TS/extract call-resolution and id-collision fixes, an Ollama context-size fix, and a clustering label-retention fix. The AST cache schema is bumped, so the first build after upgrading re-extracts.

  • Fix: MCP graph_stats no longer hangs under a stdio host on Windows (and no longer silently drops its built-at-commit staleness line): _git_head closes the git child's stdin so git does not block on the JSON-RPC pipe (#4440, #4441, thanks @bercedev).
  • Fix: Rust a use crate::…::Name edge now lands on the declaration it imports rather than a bare name-folded target (#4439, #4443, thanks @bercedev).
  • Fix: JS/TS a file-scope callback's own parameters are now shadowed, so a bare parameter passed on as a call argument no longer mints phantom cross-bundle indirect_call edges (#4448, #4457, thanks @bercedev).
  • Fix: extract a module symbol whose id spells a sibling file's id (a const sql beside x.sql) is kept on its own node instead of being merged onto the sibling's file node and dropped (#4449, #4452, thanks @Deepjyoti-Sarmah).
  • Fix: TypeScript a class field's receiver type is now scoped to its own class and bases, so this.field.method() resolves to the right class when two classes share a field name (#4294, #4297, thanks @rohit-jsfreaky).
  • Fix: Ollama extraction and labeling requests now go to Ollama's native /api/chat, so the derived num_ctx actually reaches the server and large prompts are no longer silently truncated; a server without /api/chat falls back to the OpenAI-compatible endpoint (#4413, #4414, thanks @smngvlkz).
  • Fix: cluster a community keeps its curated or LLM label across a rebuild when its membership still overlaps the previous community by a strict majority (#2298, #4451, thanks @SrijanSriv).
  • Test: ingest the skip-existing symlink test now compares the link target against what readlink returned, fixing a false failure on Windows (#4435, #4442, thanks @bercedev).

can1357/oh-my-pi ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v18.11.0 Stable2026-10-14T22:01:49Z

@oh-my-pi/pi-agent-core

Breaking Changes

  • Removed ApprovalDetailsOptions; formatApprovalDetails takes only args again, since the compact approval prompt no longer shows tool details (#15642 by @H4vC)

Added

  • AgentTool.delegationGuidance: instructions a same-name extension replacement inherits in its description when it delegates to the native tool (#15477 by @iliaal)
  • CompactionPreparation.lastSummarizedEntryId and CompactionResult.providerReplayThroughEntryId: a remote compaction that could only fit the history before the kept tail reports the last entry its native history covers; later entries replay normally (#15712 by @H4vC)

Fixed

  • Fixed native (OpenAI/Codex) remote compaction refusing or truncating sessions that fit the window: its preflight counted the JSON-escaped request, inflating quote-, backslash- and newline-heavy tool output such as disassembly by ~10–20%; it now counts text as the model reads it (#15712 by @H4vC)
  • Fixed OpenAI V1/V2 remote compaction failing outright when the history, kept tail included, exceeds the context window (a large final response or tool output after the last check); it retries on the history before the kept tail (#15712 by @H4vC)
  • Fixed fitted output caps overflowing the context window on strict hosts (llama.cpp-family) after turns with many parallel tool results; chat-template framing is now charged per locally counted message (#15699)
  • Fixed leaked DeepSeek DSML markup reaching saved history from reasoning blocks and from turns that also made a valid tool call (#15485)

@oh-my-pi/pi-ai

Fixed

  • Fixed parallel tool calls failing with "Tool call arguments are not valid JSON" on some OpenAI-compatible gateways, such as Gemini (#15682 by @Zzz141592653)
  • Fixed Anthropic fallback-credit redemption losing its credit on errors that only mention 400 in their text, such as an overload message (#15702 by @H4vC)
  • Fixed Anthropic fallback-credit redemption sending an extra fallback-credit-2026-06-01 beta when the refused request used a newer one (#15702 by @H4vC)
  • Fixed Anthropic fallback-credit redemptions failing when the refused turn included unsigned thinking (#15702 by @H4vC)
  • Fixed turns ending without automatic retry when a gateway forwards Go's use of closed network connection transport error; it now retries like other dropped connections (#15385)
  • Fixed Codex SSE streams failing with an abort instead of retrying when the first event stalls (#15542)
  • Fixed Codex (ChatGPT subscription) requests with reasoning disabled, such as judges, titles, commit messages, and thinking off, leaving effort to the server default; they now send the lowest supported effort, or none where the model accepts it (#15514)
  • Fixed a Codex account whose token OpenAI rejects (401/403) continuing to show its last quota as healthy; omp usage now drops the stale report and omp auth-gateway check reports the credential as failing (#15439)
  • Fixed usage reports being served indefinitely while their provider keeps failing; the last successful report is now shown for at most 24 hours after it was fetched (#15439)
  • Fixed Responses requests 400ing (DeepSeek: "The reasoning_text in the thinking mode must be passed back to the API") after a tool call whose arguments were invalid JSON: the call is now replayed with its parse diagnostic instead of being dropped and leaving a trailing [Orphan tool result] note (#15568)
  • Fixed DeepSeek on OpenAI-compatible gateways leaving doubled-pipe DSML markup (</｜｜DSML｜｜parameter>) in assistant text and reasoning (#15485)
  • Fixed sessions failing every request with 400 … thinking or redacted_thinking blocks in the latest assistant message cannot be modified after a response fell back to another model mid-turn (e.g. through a gateway that opts into Anthropic server-side fallback); the turn now replays one model's reasoning, and a session already stuck on such a turn recovers by dropping that turn's reasoning and retrying (#15654 by @abilliontokens)
  • Fixed omp auth-gateway's Anthropic /v1/messages stream misnumbering content blocks after a redacted reasoning block, which misrouted every later delta in index-addressed clients (#15654 by @abilliontokens)

@oh-my-pi/pi-catalog

Added

  • Added local/nemotron-streaming-en-0.6b for local streaming English dictation (#15759 by @daaximus).
  • Added SambaNova Cloud (sambanova) as a provider: /login sambanova with an API key, and the live /v1/models roster with its prices, reasoning models, and Gemma 4 image input (#14986 by @rjuez00)

Changed

  • OpenCode Go requests now go to the current https://opencode.ai/inference/go/v1 endpoint instead of the legacy /zen/go one (#15722)

Fixed

  • Fixed OpenCode Go's deepseek-flash alias showing $0, unknown limits, and no reasoning instead of the V4.1 Flash rate card and capabilities (#15357)
  • Fixed OpenCode Go's Gemini 4 Argon Contributor failing with an unsupported protocol and thinking level; it now uses the native Gemini endpoint (#15722)

@oh-my-pi/pi-coding-agent

Added

  • Added a microphone button beside Send in Tern: click to record, click Stop to finish, then review and submit the transcription yourself. Clicking while it says Starting… cancels a slow first model load (#15759 by @daaximus).
  • Added local Nemotron streaming dictation: words appear while you speak, and the microphone waits for the recognizer to be ready before recording (#15759 by @daaximus).
  • Added agent frontmatter structured: true: that agent's task spawns keep the yield tool and its output schema validation while task.structured is off (#15717 by @alphastorm)
  • The composer stays usable while a tool approval is pending: sending a message denies the call and steers the turn with it, so the agent knows why it was denied (#15642 by @H4vC)
  • Session entries record which omp build wrote them, so a transcript shows whether a fix was live even in a session left open across an upgrade: the version, plus commit, branch, and uncommitted-diff hash when run from a source checkout, and OMP_BUILD_LABEL when set. /session lists the builds that wrote the session (#15680 by @will-bogusz)

Changed

  • The in-composer tool approval prompt summarizes the command, path, or content to one row so the composer keeps its room; Ctrl+O shows them in full (#15642 by @H4vC)
  • Background task guidance now lives in the Task tool description (and in same-name extension replacements that delegate to Task) instead of being repeated in every spawn acknowledgment (#15477 by @iliaal)
  • The Auto-Promote Context and Retry Model Fallback settings now state that they are independent; turn off Retry Model Fallback (retry.modelFallback) to stop retry.fallbackChains model switches (#15636)

Fixed

  • Fixed browser tab.click("text/…") timing out when the match was below the fold or the tab was in the background (#14864 by @alnaggar-dev, #15496 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed browser clicks the page never received, such as on a disabled control or inside an inert subtree, reporting success; the call now fails saying the browser did not deliver the click (#15496 by @will-bogusz)
  • tab.ariaSnapshot() no longer prints the value of a password field; it shows one bullet per character, as tab.observe() and the screen do, so a typed or browser-autofilled password stays out of the model's context (#15502 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed project discovery loading plugins, SYSTEM.md, rules, context files, and skills from a .omp at a shared temporary root (/tmp, /var/tmp, the temp dir) above an unanchored working directory, or, when HOME is overridden, from another account's ~/.omp outside the working directory's git repository (#15719 by @alphastorm)
  • Fixed browser tab.text() and tab.html() (and the same calls on a frame) throwing when called without a selector; they now read the page body on Chrome, Tern and cmux (#15599 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed skill-description compression re-sending the same smol-model requests every session for a skill whose compressed answer was rejected (over 12 words or 160 characters) or cut off at the token limit; that model is no longer asked again for that skill (a newly selected smol model is), and compressions still running at exit get up to 4 seconds to save (#13291, #15720 by @alphastorm)
  • Fixed the macOS computer tool keeping the display awake for the rest of the session after a native call hung; the call's timeout or closing the session now lets it sleep (#15414 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed an agent definition whose tools value names no tool (tools: "", a number, a list without strings) getting every default built-in tool; it now gets none, like tools: [] (#15718 by @alphastorm)
  • Fixed page.waitForNetworkIdle in a browser run timing out on a request an earlier run started (#15495 by @alnaggar-dev and @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed a tab that a relay page opens over its own CDP session (Target.createTarget) landing in a separate Chrome window with newWindow, under an id omp could not attach to, activate or close; it now opens as a relay tab in that page's browser (#15494 by @alnaggar-dev and @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed an Anthropic refusal fallback redeeming its fallback credit with an outdated system prompt and tools after switching models; it now sends a fresh request (#15702 by @H4vC)
  • Fixed OpenAI/Codex auto-compaction failing with "Remote compaction input exceeds the context window" after a turn's final response pushed history past the window; native compaction now compacts the history before the kept recent turns, which stay in context as-is (#15712 by @H4vC)
  • Fixed omp commit, /git, and other agent commits skipping the signature when Git's commit.gpgsign is enabled; they now sign with the configured key, and a signing failure leaves HEAD unchanged (#14810)
  • Fixed rejected task calls appearing successful to SDK hosts instead of reporting a tool error (#15573)
  • Fixed read claiming regenerated images were identical when their pixels changed at the same path (#15668)
  • Fixed SDK embedders leaking every disposed AgentSession that owned its MCP manager, so long-lived processes that create and dispose many sessions grew memory without bound (#15386)
  • Fixed retry model fallback stopping at a fallback model whose provider is outside a subagent's task.agentAccountPools pool; that model is now skipped and the next fallback model is tried (#15423)
  • Fixed the system prompt's ## MCP Server Instructions section following MCP connection order, so a server connecting late or reconnecting no longer reorders it and rewrites the prompt cache; servers are now listed by name (#15705)
  • Fixed read reporting an HTTP 204 No Content response as a failed fetch; it now returns an empty successful read (#15667)
  • Fixed /mcp reauth failing with Could not discover OAuth endpoints when a server's 401 challenge is a bare Bearer resource_metadata="…"; the advertised protected-resource metadata URL is now used (#15690)
  • Fixed extension agent_end events reporting a background-job wait as a scheduled continuation; they now carry awaitingAsyncWork: true alongside willContinue (#15348)
  • Fixed auto-compaction waiting out provider retry delays longer than retry.maxDelayMs (e.g. a 16-hour retry-after); it now tries the next configured method or reports the failure (#15311)
  • Fixed read skill://<name> for a name no loaded skill owns hanging for the rest of the turn while an MCP server was still connecting or reconnecting; it now reports Unknown skill after at most 5 seconds (#15648)
  • Fixed /new ignoring "Start in Plan Mode" (plan.defaultOnStartup): the new session now starts in plan mode like the first one (#15627)
  • Fixed Cursor models' Read on an image file returning the file's raw bytes as text; Read now loads the image, and Cursor's text-only Read frame gets a short image note instead of the bytes (#15630)
  • Fixed Mermaid diagrams showing &lt;, &gt;, &amp;, and #quot; literally inside labels instead of <, >, &, and " (#15619)
  • Fixed background job results already consumed by an eval agent() handle's wait() being re-injected as one async-result follow-up when the cell ran past the 5-minute job retention window (#15625)
  • Fixed /tan missing the parent's prompt cache: it advertised the parent's xd:// tools as top-level tools and dropped ask, so its tool list no longer matched (#15578)
  • Fixed forks and reloaded sessions missing the prompt cache after a compaction in a session with eval history (#15695)
  • Fixed compiled-binary extensions importing @oh-my-pi/pi-tui subpaths such as terminal-capabilities, chat/svg-figure or theme/index loading a second copy from their own node_modules instead of sharing the host's terminal, theme and figure state (#15659)
  • Fixed marketplace plugins that keep their version in .claude-plugin/plugin.json never being reported by omp plugin upgrade or auto-updated; for plugins shipped inside the marketplace, plugin.json's version now also takes precedence over the catalog entry's, as in Claude Code (#15585)
  • Fixed a steer (queued user or peer message) waiting behind a bash service's readiness wait (name + ready) for up to ready.timeout; the call now returns early with the service still starting, inspectable via proc://<name> (#15653)
  • Fixed omp install/omp plugin install, upgrade, uninstall and doctor --fix failing with Executable not found in $PATH: "bun" for npm and Git plugins on machines without Bun installed, such as standalone-binary installs (#15480)
  • Fixed /plugins enable and /plugins disable in the TUI not refreshing skills, commands, task agents and MCP servers until a reload, and rejecting npm plugins (#15383)
  • Fixed local memory injection and learn using the Settings directory instead of the session's project directory, so SDK hosts sharing one Settings across projects injected another project's lessons and never the project's consolidated summary; sharpshooter memory follows the session directory too (#15375)
  • Fixed ACP bash approvals ignoring bash.patterns, and an earlier "Always allow" letting critical commands such as rm -rf / run without a prompt (#15473)
  • Fixed hashline edits silently dropping the tail of a line that read or grep had cut at the column limit; such an edit is now refused until a :raw read shows the full line (#15649)
  • Fixed isolated tasks failing on linked worktrees with initialized recursive submodules and leaving updated submodule pins unreachable after cleanup (#15457)
  • Fixed /tan missing the parent's prompt cache by advertising xd:// tools top-level and omitting ask (#15578).

@oh-my-pi/pi-mnemopi

Fixed

  • Fixed polyphonic recall ranking unrelated memories above relevant ones when the query shared only a common word such as "that" with an extracted fact subject (#15512).

@oh-my-pi/pi-natives

Fixed

  • Fixed macOS setValue on native text fields in background apps such as Contacts, Reminders, Calendar, Font Book and System Settings returning success for text the app showed but never stored: it now enters the text the way win.type does, and a mismatch names what the field reads (#15293 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed a macOS desktop session keeping the display awake for the rest of the process after a native request hung; the request's timeout or close() now releases it (#15414 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed the macOS computer tool sometimes reading an empty page on its first read of a Chrome window, and leaving Electron apps it read in screen-reader mode until they quit (#15604 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed macOS press() on a scrolled-out row in a Mac Catalyst app reporting success while nothing happened: the element is asked to scroll into view first (#15607 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed shell builtins on Windows resolving drive-relative paths (C:, C:name) against the agent process's start directory instead of the shell's working directory, which let cd /c/Temp/x && rm -rf C: delete the start directory; rm now also refuses a bare drive operand like . (#15511)
  • Fixed renderMermaidAscii printing character references in labels verbatim: HTML entities (&lt;, &amp;, &#60;, &#x3C;) and Mermaid entity codes (#quot;, #60;) now render as the characters they name (#15619)
  • Fixed read and while read hanging on process-substitution input (#15501)
  • Fixed standalone binaries extracting unused native addon variants on first launch (#15462)
  • Fixed Git diffs failing when staged or committed changes update a submodule pin (#15457)

@oh-my-pi/omp-stats

Fixed

  • Providers → Subscription windows no longer mixes Codex 5-hour and 7-day quotas across plans: Team and weekly-only (ProLite) accounts now share the 7-day row, the 5-hour row counts only accounts that have one, and labels no longer flip with whichever account reported last (#15664)

@oh-my-pi/pi-tui

Added

  • Added native composer microphone controls with visible starting, recording and transcription states; a start still loading its model can be cancelled, and Enter and Send wait while dictation is recording or transcribing into the draft (#15759 by @daaximus).
  • ApprovalDialogComponent takes a draft option that routes typing to the composer beneath it, so a message can be written while a tool call waits (#15642 by @H4vC)
  • ApprovalDialogComponent takes a fullPrompt option that the expand key (app.tools.expand) or a Details button swaps in for the summarized prompt; it replaces wrapDetails (#15642 by @H4vC)

Fixed

  • SVG fences in CRLF or CR-only assistant Markdown now render as figures instead of source code (#15663).
  • Fixed clipboard-image and image-path pastes being dropped while the composer draft is edited beneath an ask or approval dialog (#15642 by @H4vC)
  • Fixed inline images inside tmux assuming Kitty graphics from the pane's TERM; the attached client is identified from tmux's terminal-type reply or its TERM (e.g. xterm-kitty), and unrecognized clients go to the SIXEL probe (#15524).
  • Fixed ```svg fences rendering as their source code instead of a drawing when no theme was installed yet, e.g. in an extension rendering a message that opens with the fence (#15660)
  • Fixed pastes inside tmux 3.5a and older submitting line by line after another program turned bracketed paste off on the pane; omp now re-enables it on the next render or keypress (#15596)
  • Fixed assistant replies containing a table (on image-capable terminals with table charts on) publishing still-streaming rows to native scrollback, which froze the block's append-only retirement and could leave overwritten reply rows in scrollback (#15693)
  • Fixed streamed thinking publishing a line to native scrollback just before a code fence or <!-- --> sentinel split across deltas rewrote it with the prose-fold ellipsis (#15693)
  • Fixed streamed assistant text or thinking containing a tab stopping all later rows of the reply from retiring into native scrollback until the turn ended (#15693)
  • Fixed wrapped lines in Write, Read, Edit, Todo, Ask and LSP tool cards losing their alignment under line numbers, tree connectors, and option or code rails (#15677)
  • Fixed SIXEL images leaving their previous raster on screen, drawn again over itself, when the transcript moves them within the viewport (#15657)

[3.0.0-alpha.5]

New Features

  • 7cf5d9032 (#16198 by @Vishal-770) Add bundle > linux > deb > compression config option to control Debian bundle compression type and level.
  • 0e849336a (#16261 by @github-actions) zstd compression for deb and rpm bundles accepts negative levels (down to -131072) for zstd's fast modes. DebCompression::Zstd's level is now an i32, and DebCompression::{default_level, min_level, max_level, level} return Option<i32>.

Dependencies

  • Upgraded to tauri-bundler@3.0.0-alpha.4
  • Upgraded to tauri-utils@3.0.0-alpha.4
  • Upgraded to tauri-macos-sign@3.0.0-alpha.3

Breaking Changes

  • 0e849336a (#16261 by @github-actions) RPM bundles are compressed with zstd level 19 instead of gzip level 6 when bundle > linux > rpm > compression is not set, which matches rpm's own default. zstd payloads need rpm 4.14 or newer, so set the option to gzip to keep installing on older distributions.

@tauri-apps/cli-v3.0.0-alpha.5@tauri-apps/cli v3.0.0-alpha.5 Stable2026-10-14T21:19:13Z

[3.0.0-alpha.5]

New Features

  • 7cf5d9032 (#16198 by @Vishal-770) Add bundle > linux > deb > compression config option to control Debian bundle compression type and level.
  • 0e849336a (#16261 by @github-actions) zstd compression for deb and rpm bundles accepts negative levels (down to -131072) for zstd's fast modes. DebCompression::Zstd's level is now an i32, and DebCompression::{default_level, min_level, max_level, level} return Option<i32>.

Dependencies

  • Upgraded to tauri-cli@3.0.0-alpha.5

Breaking Changes

  • 0e849336a (#16261 by @github-actions) RPM bundles are compressed with zstd level 19 instead of gzip level 6 when bundle > linux > rpm > compression is not set, which matches rpm's own default. zstd payloads need rpm 4.14 or newer, so set the option to gzip to keep installing on older distributions.

View release →

[3.0.0-alpha.6]

New Features

  • a7b81d993 (#16225 by @mayocream) Allow CEF windowless browsers with an application-provided render handler and native window event callback. Applications provide painting, input, IME, cursor handling and composition.

Bug Fixes

  • d78cc6085 (#16241 by @beanow-at-crabnebula) Fixed CEF child processes ignoring Cef::cef_api_version. Renderer, GPU and utility processes now declare the same CEF API version as the browser process, which passes it on each child's command line as --tauri-cef-api-version=<n>.
  • 6a2cb9688 Fixed the app reopening hidden copies of its previous run's windows, which kept the process alive after every app window was closed. After a crash or a kill, Chromium's session restore reopened the last session's tabs in Chrome browser windows the runtime does not own. The runtime now sets the session.restore_on_startup profile preference to open the New Tab page, which restores nothing. Cef::profile_preference_value can still override it.
  • c6b974b8a (#16275 by @github-actions) Fix overlay titlebars intercepting webview mouse events on macOS.
  • a5e3a697e (#16240 by @beanow-at-crabnebula) Report an unsupported CEF API version instead of crashing. The runtime now checks the result of cef_api_hash: the browser process logs the version and fails with Error::WebviewRuntimeNotInstalled, and a helper process panics with the same message.

  • Upgraded to tauri-utils@3.0.0-alpha.4
  • Upgraded to tauri-runtime@3.0.0-alpha.4

  • Upgraded to tauri@3.0.0-alpha.5
  • 370ea9769 Bump CEF to 154.5.0+154.0.34.

</details>

[3.0.0-alpha.4]

New Features

  • 7cf5d9032 (#16198 by @Vishal-770) Add bundle > linux > deb > compression config option to control Debian bundle compression type and level.
  • 0e849336a (#16261 by @github-actions) zstd compression for deb and rpm bundles accepts negative levels (down to -131072) for zstd's fast modes. DebCompression::Zstd's level is now an i32, and DebCompression::{default_level, min_level, max_level, level} return Option<i32>.

Bug Fixes

  • a5e3a697e (#16240 by @beanow-at-crabnebula) Report an unsupported CEF API version instead of crashing. The runtime now checks the result of cef_api_hash: the browser process logs the version and fails with Error::WebviewRuntimeNotInstalled, and a helper process panics with the same message.

Dependencies

  • Upgraded to tauri-utils@3.0.0-alpha.4
  • Upgraded to tauri-macos-sign@3.0.0-alpha.3

Breaking Changes

  • 0e849336a (#16261 by @github-actions) RPM bundles are compressed with zstd level 19 instead of gzip level 6 when bundle > linux > rpm > compression is not set, which matches rpm's own default. zstd payloads need rpm 4.14 or newer, so set the option to gzip to keep installing on older distributions.

New Features

  • 0e849336a (#16261 by @github-actions) zstd compression for deb and rpm bundles accepts negative levels (down to -131072) for zstd's fast modes. DebCompression::Zstd's level is now an i32, and DebCompression::{default_level, min_level, max_level, level} return Option<i32>.

[2.12.4]

Enhancements

  • 5d1e8777a (#16286) Improve the error message on iOS when the development server cannot be reached through a localhost URL, suggesting tauri ios dev --host.

Bug Fixes

  • 9c3fb8865 (#16197) Fix a panic in the IPC and asset protocol handlers when the request URI has an empty path (for example ipc:foo).
  • 7b673ddaa (#16146) PluginHandle::run_mobile_plugin_async and PluginHandle::run_mobile_plugin no longer panic (which aborts the process on iOS and Android) when a mobile plugin command resolves after its caller stopped waiting, for example when the async call was cancelled by a timeout. The late response is dropped instead.

[2.7.3]

Enhancements

  • e140a1c8f (#16280) Build-time ACL validation now suggests the closest known identifier when a capability references an unknown permission that looks like a typo of an existing one, for example Permission fs:allow-read-text-fil not found (did you mean "fs:allow-read-text-file"?), expected one of .... Typos in the plugin namespace and a missing allow- prefix are also covered; unrelated identifiers keep the previous message.

Bug Fixes

  • 225fb205a (#16247) Switch back to static_vcruntime crate, this fixes a linking error when the crate using tauri_build::build is used as library.

  • Upgraded to tauri-utils@2.10.2

[2.10.3]

Bug Fixes

  • 5a6149898 (#16227) output_ok used to throw away the stderr it captured, so a failing bundler script surfaced as a bare "failed to run" with no diagnosis (#3055). The stderr (or stdout, for tools such as WiX and signtool that report errors there) is now part of the error, and when the DMG script dies because macOS denied the terminal permission to control Finder, the error says where to enable it: System Settings > Privacy & Security > Automation.
  • d62e5d9c0 (#16184) Fix NSIS installer leaving uninstall.exe and the installation directory behind when uninstalling an existing installation from the installer.

Dependencies

  • Upgraded to tauri-utils@2.10.2
  • Upgraded to tauri-macos-sign@2.4.2
  • 5a2953e55 (#16237) Updated NSIS to 3.13
  • 55883af3f (#15639) Updated rpm-rs crate to v0.30 which should improve build times for .rpm bundles.

[2.4.2]

Bug Fixes

  • b9487627b (#15844) Fixed importing a signing certificate failing with MAC verification failed during PKCS12 import for p12 files that use modern PBES2/AES encryption. The certificate format is now detected and passed to security import explicitly (PKCS#12 or a PEM bundle), and import failures explain the expected certificate format and password.

Dependencies

  • 5a3cbfcdc (#16279) Breaking Change: Temporarily inlined the code we need from apple-codesign-rs until its next release to unblock important dependency updates. Technically this is a breaking change to the CertificateProfile enum re-export and the Error::FailedToCreateSelfSignedCertificate enum variant.

coreyhaines31/marketingskills ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v2.11.30v2.11.30 — Lessons from Railway's agent-first growth, Wispr Flow's creator ad program, and recent h… Stable2026-10-14T21:21:00Z

Lessons from Railway's agent-first growth, Wispr Flow's creator ad program, and recent hook and X-listening threads, added to existing skills.

  • ai-seo (2.7.6 → 2.8.0): references/agent-defaults.md covers products an agent installs mid-task. It explains how to measure which vendor Claude Code, Cursor, and Codex pick per stack, which stall points to fix, and the levers that move the default (no-signup provisioning, agent setup, per-stack task pages, migration guides). It also rules out agent-facing instructions that tell agents to choose you.
  • influencer-marketing (1.2.0 → 1.3.0): references/paid-ads-creator-program.md covers creators paid a share of the ad spend behind their videos. It includes rate math, the guaranteed monthly minimum, recruiting a small cohort, staged growth with a cap, coaching, and four-part briefs.
  • ads (2.4.7 → 2.5.0): the Meta playbook adds creator ads at volume. Near-identical ads get clustered, so plan by concept, angle, and awareness level, optimize for a quality event, and test the funnel in Instagram's in-app browser. AI variant farming now notes that close variants count as one concept. LinkedIn retargeting is now sequenced by behavior.
  • ad-creative (2.11.0 → 2.12.0): the hook system adds the contrast family (choice, old vs. new, us vs. them, false assumption, declaration then question) and how it differs from the "it's not X, it's Y" AI tell.
  • social (2.3.4 → 2.4.0): contrast hooks in the short-form library, and X-through-Grok jobs in the listening reference.
  • marketing-loops (1.4.0 → 1.5.0): agent-default check, niche-outlier, creator-scouting, and launch-reaction loops. The catalog now has 56 loops.

Skill ZIP assets

Complete source catalog: 50 skills. Claude web target: 50 skills with reviewed short descriptions.

Choose <name>-claude-web.zip for the documented single-folder web upload format. Source ZIPs retain original metadata; they are not substitutes for omitted web ZIPs. Live client upload, account limits, and invocation behavior have not been verified.
