DioxusLabs/dioxus ↗ 1 release · 1 prerelease
v0.8.0-alpha.2 Prerelease2026-10-14T20:53:40Z

Highlights

  • New template and rendering internals (#5554):
    • Dioxus no longer uses comment-node placeholders and markers, either at runtime or during hydration. Elements that treat their children as text, such as textarea, now render correctly.
    • rsx! now expands to a const builder API, which you can also use directly.
    • HTML elements are trait-based, and you can define your own with dioxus::html::define_elements!.
    • Core now supports renderer IDs. These lay the groundwork for portals and multiple windows sharing one runtime.
  • Dioxus Native is updated to Blitz 0.3.0-beta.3 (#5780, #5909). This release also fixes a leak where removed nodes were never freed (#5911). A falsy boolean attribute like disabled: false is now removed instead of being written as "false" (#5913). Attributes now default to the empty namespace (#5851).
  • Linux desktop apps no longer link libxdo by default (#5749). This fixes prebuilt binaries failing to start on distros that ship a different libxdo soname.
  • CLI:
    • The bundle application name now comes from application.name in Dioxus.toml (#5533).
    • The runtime no-downloads setting is now respected when bundling (#5748).
    • Fixed a wasm-opt SIGABRT when stripping binaries (#5736).
    • Fixed wasm hot-patching with wasm-bindgen 0.2.128 and newer (#5843).
    • The dev server proxy now sets X-Forwarded-Host (#5627).

Breaking changes

  • HTML elements are no longer namespaced under dioxus_elements (#5554). Because element builders are now ordinary items, they can clash with other names in scope. For example, write html::main {} instead of main {} when your crate also has a main function.
  • libxdo is now opt-in on Linux (#5749). If your app uses the predefined Copy, Cut, Paste or Select All menu items, enable the linux-libxdo feature on dioxus-desktop. That means adding dioxus-desktop to your dependencies directly, because the feature isn't exposed through dioxus.

What's Changed

  • Lock cargo-msrv installation dependencies in CI by @danielkov in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5728
  • Remove CODEOWNERS by @nicoburns in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5727
  • Fix leak in manganis iOS FFI wrappers: hold Retained<AnyObject> instead of a raw pointer by @nicoburns in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5733
  • feat(cli): Set bundle application name from config by @adamgreg in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5533
  • feat(desktop): make libxdo opt-in on Linux via linux-libxdo by @nabbisen in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5749
  • fix(bundle): honor runtime no-downloads setting by @subotac in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5748
  • Fix manganis-macro resolve_missing_file_path test on Windows by @jkelleyrtp in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5762
  • Markerless placeholders/Portals/Multiwindow/const builder/optimized templates by @ealmloff in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5554
  • Upgrade to Blitz 0.3.0-beta.2 by @nicoburns in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5780
  • Skip stable fragment edge scans when no children need placement by @ealmloff in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5802
  • Set X-Forwarded-Host in the proxy by @paholg in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5627
  • fix(rsx): quote_spanned! -> quote!. Resolves clippy::useless_format lints. by @scottfones in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5636
  • Fix windows-hotpatch-fullstack flake: route LaunchedRoot through Subsecond; remove SymbolData probe log spam by @nicoburns in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5790
  • cli: mirror Cargo's implicit strip default when manually stripping binaries (fixes wasm-opt SIGABRT) by @nicoburns in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5736
  • lychee: exclude freyaui.dev (403 from bot protection) by @nicoburns in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5844
  • fix(liveview): don't double the ? and # when reading the browser location by @Dev-next-gen in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5836
  • Use the empty namespace by default on HTML attributes by @hovinen in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5851
  • Introduce a function to create a synthetic form event by @hovinen in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5750
  • ci: fix Linux apt package install (stale apt lists + poisoned cache), fix cargo msrv find by @nicoburns in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5873
  • fix(router-macro): omit ? and stray & when query arguments are empty by @nicoburns in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5845
  • cli: fix wasm hot-patch fat builds with wasm-bindgen 0.2.128 / Rust beta (Playwright CI) by @nicoburns in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5843
  • fix: Filter crates that are not in target by @nicoburns in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5903
  • Eliminate quote/proc-macro2 deps from dioxus-config-macro crate by @nicoburns in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5868
  • Sync Dioxus Native (Blitz 0.3.0-beta.3) by @nicoburns in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5909

zed-industries/zed ↗ 2 releases · 1 stable · 1 prerelease
v1.25.0-pre Prerelease2026-10-14T16:43:17Z

This week's release includes automatic unfolding of folded code when jumping to a location, applying inlay hint edits with a double-click, context compaction for external agents, and new Gemini 4 Argon and Amazon Bedrock models.

Features

AI

  • Added the Gemini 4 Argon model (gemini-4-argon) to the Google AI provider. (#65565; thanks LorenzoBiassio)
  • Added GPT-6 Astra, Claude Fable 5.1, and Claude Opus 5.5 to the Amazon Bedrock provider. (#64201, #64685; thanks IbrahimKhan12 and alexandergunnarson)
  • Enabled session notices and context compaction for external agents using ACP. (#65414)

Git

  • Improved the branch picker to make clear that deleting a remote-tracking branch only removes the local ref, and removed the force delete option for remote-tracking branches, since it had no effect. (#61793; thanks lld4n)

Languages

  • Added support for applying inlay hint text edits by double-clicking a hint, or by running editor: accept inlay hint with the cursor at the hint. (#64789)

Remote Development

  • Improved SSH remote connection time by detecting the remote shell, platform, and OS version in one command instead of three, which helps most when connecting from Windows. (#65065; thanks Huliiiiii)

Other

  • Added automatic unfolding of folded sections when jumping to a location via go to line, go to definition/declaration/implementation/type definition, symbol navigation, the outline and project symbol pickers, multibuffer location results, and navigation history. (#65217; thanks henne90gen)
  • Added an "Open SVG Preview" option to the Project Panel's context menu for SVG files. (#65539; thanks skmanoj322)
  • Added the tab_switcher.show_file_preview setting, which controls whether files are previewed in the editor while cycling through the tab switcher (defaults to true). (#56235; thanks sebascadpi)
  • Improved the tab switcher so it no longer changes selection on mouse hover and no longer delays showing its popover; you can still click entries to switch tabs. (#65546)
  • Improved startup time when restoring hundreds of editor selections. (#65171)
  • Improved startup time when using custom icon themes. (#65173)
  • Reduced Zed's binary size by about 33% on macOS, 28% on Windows, and 7% on Linux. (#65168)

Bug Fixes

  • Fixed a bug where environment variables, including secrets, passed to external agents and MCP servers in remote projects were visible in the local machine's process list. (#65174)
  • Fixed prompts to external agents being slow to send, or never sending, because of unneeded Git checkpoints. (#65304)
  • Fixed the remote server failing to start when connecting to a Windows host over SSH. (#55292; thanks sergio-u)
  • Fixed ctrl-o and ctrl-i in Vim mode not navigating back and forward when a Markdown preview was focused. (#65299; thanks gitKashish)
  • Fixed a bug where archiving an agent thread did nothing and kept a CPU core busy when the thread's linked worktree folder had been deleted. (#65578)
  • Fixed a bug where case conversion and other text transformation commands duplicated the word when multiple cursors were inside it. (#65291; thanks gitKashish)
  • Fixed a bug where navigating back skipped a Markdown preview tab after switching to another file. (#65299; thanks gitKashish)
  • Fixed a bug where notification sounds could stop playing after an audio output error, and on Linux an audio thread could keep using a full CPU core until Zed restarted. (#65158)
  • Fixed a bug where the Git Panel's commit editor buttons changed opacity when hovering over empty space. (#65448; thanks shuvroroy)
  • Fixed a bug where the Project Panel's "Open Markdown Preview" option did not appear for files opened on their own (e.g. zed README.md). (#65539; thanks skmanoj322)
  • Fixed a bug where the Settings UI sidebar did not highlight the subsection being scrolled through, including the last short subsections of a page. (#65172; thanks mayurathavale18)
  • Fixed a visual artifact at the corners of scrollable code blocks when hovered. (#64978)
  • Fixed blurred window backgrounds not blurring on macOS 27. (#65361; thanks biw)
  • Fixed edit predictions showing an unlimited allowance and available controls when a team organization with no active subscription was selected. (#65301)
  • Fixed external agents' first updates in a new thread sometimes being dropped, and agent error output being missing when an agent exited during startup. (#65339)
  • Fixed extra space between the scrollbar and the header of terminals in the Agent Panel. (#65358; thanks chrisdrackett)
  • Fixed Gemini models failing with an error after a tool call returned an image. (#65576)
  • Fixed hover popovers flickering or closing too early on mouse movement with language servers that omit range information. (#64032; thanks daydalek)
  • Fixed images inside center- or right-aligned HTML blocks rendering left-aligned in Markdown Preview. (#63166; thanks pray3m)
  • Fixed OpenAI-compatible edit predictions failing on servers that limit stop sequences to 4. (#65360; thanks mischah)
  • Fixed search not working in the debugger's terminal, opened with cmd-f (macOS) or ctrl-shift-f (Linux/Windows). (#56863; thanks iamsushank)
  • Fixed text finder queries filled in from the current selection being treated as a regular expression instead of literal text when regex mode was on. (#65321; thanks Syozik)
  • Fixed the Collab Panel's empty-state styling not matching the Git Panel. (#65290; thanks arondubovszky)
  • Fixed wrapped links in Markdown tables being partially unclickable in Markdown previews and agent responses. (#65366; thanks julian-bruyers)

v1.24.2 Stable2026-10-14T16:43:09Z

This week's release includes Git tag creation, the ability to drag Terminal tabs into the Agent Panel, and pre-filling of the File Finder's search query with text selected in the editor or terminal.

Features

AI

  • Improved Agent Panel terminal support by allowing Terminal tabs to be dragged into it. (#60582; thanks cppcoffee)
  • Improved custom Amazon Bedrock model support with configurable tool use, images, and thinking. (#64953; thanks mpyuglgwkxcmodyrpo)
  • Improved memory and CPU usage while the agent responds in long threads. (#65244)

Git

  • Added a git: create tag at head command and a Create Tag… context menu action in Git history and Git Graph to create local tags on commits. Tags are not pushed automatically. (#61973, #65194; thanks cppcoffee)
  • Added gitiles as a Git hosting provider for Gitiles/Gerrit deployments, enabling permalinks and navigation from the Git blame diff view. (#63996; thanks JonathanBerkeley)
  • Added a side-by-side view for directory diffs opened with zed --diff. (#64967; thanks ian-h-chamberlain)
  • Added file counts to section headers in the Git Panel. (#65028; thanks mansoorkhan96)
  • Added self-hosted Gerrit repositories as a provider for permalinks. (#53832; thanks starsep)
  • Improved the Git Panel with a collapsible commit message editor, configurable with git_panel.commit_editor, and the Git Graph with collapsible commit details. (#63236; thanks mulhamna)
  • Improved Git Graph readability by moving refs from the description column into the graph. (#59839; thanks RemcoSmitsDev)
  • Improved Git Panel file actions for multiple selected files. (#64640; thanks Acd-kk)

Debugger

  • Improved the debugger variables view with distinct colors for values based on their type, such as integers and strings. (#64111; thanks syormon)

Languages

  • Added a Show Source option to the Markdown preview tab context menu. (#65081; thanks joshkent94)
  • Added a Go package-level test gutter button next to package declarations. (#56699; thanks cppcoffee)
  • Added support for language-server extensions to configure which language servers are enabled by default in extension.toml. See the extension documentation. (#65030)
  • Added markdown_preview.heading_font_weight to configure the font weight of headings in the Markdown preview. (#64421; thanks dremnik)
  • Added the mermaid_font_family setting to configure the font used in Mermaid diagrams. (#63430; thanks Sha1rholder)
  • Improved the column filter dropdown in the tabular data preview to sort values naturally (e.g., 2 before 10). (#64764; thanks HalavicH)

Remote Development

  • Improved remote server responsiveness when log or RPC response writes are blocked. (#65011; thanks Huliiiiii)

Linux

  • Reduced the Linux release binary size by about 23% on x86_64. (#65169)

macOS

  • Improved how Finder groups Zed on macOS by identifying it as a developer tool. (#65130; thanks kennyheard)

Other

  • Added an extension_suggestions setting to turn off extension suggestions. (#65109; thanks audinue)
  • Improved the File Finder to pre-fill its query with text selected in the editor or terminal. Disable this with file_finder.prefill_query_from_selection. (#64946; thanks AbyssWaIker)
  • Improved multi-cursor editing performance. (#64409; thanks Rani367)
  • Improved project search speed and memory usage over misclassified binary files. (#64549)
  • Improved the Extensions view so installed extensions appear even while offline or when the remote list is incomplete. (#64766)
  • Improved the font family pickers in the settings window to render each option in its own font as a preview. (#64199; thanks mizanisoffline)
  • Improved Zed startup time. (#65170)

Bug Fixes

  • Fixed .env files being treated as shell scripts and triggering shellcheck warnings. They now use the Env language. (#64242; thanks pleahmacaka)
  • Fixed a crash when editing a buffer while an inlay-hint request was pending. (#65193)
  • Fixed a crash when syntax parsing requested bytes inside a multibyte character. (#65273)
  • Fixed a ty language server panic when opening a Python file as a single-file project. (#60753; thanks MostlyKIGuess)
  • Fixed ACP session title clearing and prevented programmatic title updates from being saved as user renames. (#65195)
  • Fixed ChatGPT Subscription models disappearing when the model catalog takes longer than 5 seconds to load. (#64925; thanks dualfroz)
  • Fixed Command-clicking a symbol whose name matches its file name doing nothing. (#64892; thanks skmanoj322)
  • Fixed crashes on tab-indented code blocks. (#65406)
  • Fixed emoji, symbol, and non-Latin fonts rendering as empty boxes on Linux after being used as a font fallback. (#65269)
  • Fixed files created in a directory that was deleted and immediately recreated (for example by a build or test step that clears and repopulates an output directory) not appearing until the workspace was reloaded. (#61372; thanks marcalc)
  • Fixed files in symlinked directories opening in a separate worktree when a language server reports them by their canonical path. (#64895; thanks taburet)
  • Fixed hanging during remote project search. (#65257)

  • Fixed selected context being lost when added before an agent thread finished loading. (#64252; thanks porada)
  • Fixed the Git Panel offering to initialize a repository when a single file was opened as a project, which failed with "Not a directory". (#64948; thanks virajbhartiya)

  • Fixed macro_rules! text flickering in the Rust completion menu. (#63185; thanks mTvare6)

  • Fixed shift-enter in the JetBrains keymap not selecting the previous search match. (#65129; thanks codello)

herdrdev/herdr ↗ 2 releases · 1 stable · 1 prerelease
v0.9.5 Stable2026-10-14T16:07:28Z

This is a hotfix release for v0.9.4. See the v0.9.4 notes for the full feature release: https://github.com/herdrdev/herdr/releases/tag/v0.9.4

Fixed

  • Prefix shortcuts work again when the terminal sends the next key as plain text. This affected Tern, Chinese, Japanese and Korean input methods on macOS, and Ghostty text: keybinds that send the prefix and a key together. Workspace navigation had the same problem. (#5255)
  • Codex sessions no longer start with a "clamping Interrupt hook timeout" warning. Herdr no longer installs Codex turn hooks, and Codex status comes from screen detection again. Run herdr integration install codex to update. (#5246, #5278)
  • Claude Code questions show as blocked in named sessions. (#4573, #5192)
  • On Windows, plugin panes start even when a folder on PATH has the same name as the plugin's program. (#5267, #5269, thanks @JJLiebig)
  • On Windows, Claude Code session tracking works when the user folder name contains non-ASCII characters, such as Korean. (#5253, #5270, thanks @JJLiebig)
  • Adding a Windows machine no longer prints raw PowerShell progress output. (#4639, #5282, thanks @JJLiebig)
